# Agents · G · part 3

898 entries, gateway-route-guard to gemini-analyzer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **gateway-route-guard**: Use PROACTIVELY whenever a new page is added under app/ (e.g. a new app/&lt;name&gt;/page.tsx) that must be reachable before the user has logged in — signup, email verification, password reset, public landing pages. Also use if a page here "works in dev but redirects to home.leedohyun.com / looks like it 404s in production for logged-out users" — that symptom is almost always a missing gateway whitelist entry, not a bug in this repo's routing. ([lee-dohyun/customer.front](https://github.com/lee-dohyun/customer.front/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gateway-route-guard.md))
- **gateway-route-guard**: Use PROACTIVELY whenever a new page, route, or API endpoint is added or changed in this repo (application*.yml routes, or JwtAuthenticationFilter's PROTECTED_HOSTS/OPTIONAL_AUTH_HOSTS lists), OR whenever the sibling repos auth.api/customer.front/home.front add a new page/endpoint that must be reachable before login. Also use when a report comes in that a page under a *.leedohyun.com host "silently redirects to home.leedohyun.com" or "404s/redirects for logged-out users" — that symptom is almost always a missing gateway whitelist entry, not a routing bug in the other repo. ([lee-dohyun/gateway](https://github.com/lee-dohyun/gateway/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gateway-route-guard.md))
- **gateway-route-guard**: Use PROACTIVELY if this repo ever adds identity/auth integration, or a page here starts calling an API on a protected host (e.g. customer.leedohyun.com/api/auth/**), or `home.leedohyun.com` itself is ever added to gateway's PROTECTED_HOSTS. Also use if a page "works in dev but redirects unexpectedly / looks like it 404s in production" — that symptom pattern is almost always a missing gateway whitelist entry. ([lee-dohyun/store.front](https://github.com/lee-dohyun/store.front/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gateway-route-guard.md))
- **gateway-sentinel**: Weekly read-only exposure sweep of the review-plane attack surface: the identity-aware gateway routes and every path that could leak raw CDP (browser control), VNC/noVNC (desktop), PTY (shell), interpreter, or preview ports out of a conversation computer or Agent Sandbox. Checks Helm charts, NetworkPolicies, route/token code, and (when a read-only kubectl context is available) the live cluster. Reports findings severity-first with exact file/line or resource evidence. Never modifies code or the cluster; the caller files issues for findings. ([elewa-git/opencrane](https://github.com/elewa-git/opencrane/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gateway-sentinel.md) · ★6)
- **gateway-steward**: Executes the explicit-only gateway stewardship runbook with strict auth exclusion and proof-gated maintenance. ([miketdonahue/my-skills](https://github.com/miketdonahue/my-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/gateway-steward.md))
- **gateway-tester**: Specialized tester for end-to-end gateway simulation, benchmark testing, and UI integration checks. ([maloosatyam-google/apigee-ai-gateway-reference](https://github.com/maloosatyam-google/apigee-ai-gateway-reference/blob/HEAD/.gemini/agents/gateway-tester.md))

- **gather-data-worker**: Isolated research worker for `/gather-data-cycle`. Hosts the gather-data research flow for a SINGLE candidate in a fresh, non-contaminated context: runs the web search-fetch-verify loop and writes the candidate JSON, then exits with a structured report block. Runs in `initial` mode (full attribute set on a stub) or `backfill` mode (only the missing attributes named in the prompt, existing values untouched). Deliberately CANNOT commit and does NOT flip the RESEARCH.md checkbox — the orchestrator owns both so parallel workers never race on shared files. Spawned one-per-candidate, in parallel ba… ([mtrense/lineup](https://github.com/mtrense/lineup/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gather-data-worker.md) · ★4)

- **gatherer**: Read-only gathering over files: locate, read, extract and summarize a named list of paths. Offline, so a web dimension goes to an in-session band worker instead. Returns at most 400 words plus a path to the long version. Use for grep fan-outs, bulk read-and-summarize over a bounded scope, and doc lookups. A read-only role runs through `citizen role run &lt;role&gt;`: confinement is read roots and return shape, not the absence of write tools, so `builder` needs neither and spawns natively. ([JakeSelby/model-citizen](https://github.com/JakeSelby/model-citizen/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/gatherer.md) · ★24 · 2 copies)
- **gatherer**: Gather worker agent — spawned by lead-researcher skill with assigned language(s) and topic. Each gatherer agent focuses on its assigned language/topic and runs the search-fetch loop from the deep-gather skill. - **Gauntlet Lead**: Gauntlet-loop orchestrator — sets a real reference bar, splits the goal into independently gradeable units, dispatches builders (clean context) and blind fresh-per-round critics, loops failures until every unit beats the bar, two rounds stall, or budget runs out. The LEAD never builds and never grades. NOT challenger (verifies factual claims in an artifact), NOT /review (one-pass verdict), NOT sdlc-lead (pipeline orchestration) — this is the quality-maximization harness for "make it as good as something real we named in advance". ([bpmforge/attest](https://github.com/bpmforge/attest/blob/HEAD/agents/gauntlet-lead.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **gauntlet-ab-critic**: Blind A/B critic for a gauntlet loop. Compares two artifacts without being told which is the candidate, picks a winner, and names exactly one biggest remaining gap. ([th-yoo/gauntlet-loop](https://github.com/th-yoo/gauntlet-loop/blob/HEAD/agents/gauntlet-ab-critic.md))
- **gauntlet-adversary**: Gauntlet adversarial lens — hostile scrutiny of a frozen subject. Dispatched by the /gauntlet Workflow, parameterized by a roster persona card. Not for general use. ([ZMS-Labs/epistemic-skills](https://github.com/ZMS-Labs/epistemic-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/epistemic-skills/agents/gauntlet-adversary.md))
- **gauntlet-analyst**: Use this agent as part of The Gauntlet review process to validate game logic, TCG rules implementation, and domain correctness. This agent specializes in TCG mechanics and ensures game rules are correctly translated to code. Invoke after implementation is complete but before requesting human review. ([chroniicallydiistracted/Botcana2.0](https://github.com/chroniicallydiistracted/Botcana2.0/blob/HEAD/lorcanito-and-botcana/.claude/_legacy/agents/gauntlet-analyst.md) · 3 copies)
- **gauntlet-arbitrator**: Gauntlet arbitrator — builds the dissent-preserving Conflict Ledger and renders the computed GO/CONDITIONAL/NO-GO. Dispatched LAST by the /gauntlet Workflow on the verified lens reports; the owner designates the reviewer and the caller reports actual model/context separation. Not for general use. ([ZMS-Labs/epistemic-skills](https://github.com/ZMS-Labs/epistemic-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/epistemic-skills/agents/gauntlet-arbitrator.md))
- **gauntlet-breaker**: One-command probe for a gauntlet loop: reports whether the run token still exists, or the byte count of one named file, and nothing else. Holds no tool that can read an artifact's contents, so it cannot leak which side of a blind A/B is which. ([th-yoo/gauntlet-loop](https://github.com/th-yoo/gauntlet-loop/blob/HEAD/agents/gauntlet-breaker.md))

- **gauntlet-consumer-advocate**: Adversarial reviewer that attacks an idea from the perspective of whoever consumes it downstream — API client, library user, next service, on-call responder. Spawn a FRESH one every round and never pass it the builder's reasoning. - **gauntlet-critic**: Harsh blind critic for a gauntlet loop. Fetches the real bar and the actual work, picks A or B with identities withheld, names one gap. ([aaronwJordan/gauntlet-loop](https://github.com/aaronwJordan/gauntlet-loop/blob/HEAD/.grok/agents/gauntlet-critic.md) · 2 copies)

- **gauntlet-formalist**: Adversarial reviewer that attacks an idea on structure, logic, and invariants. - **gdbox-acceptor**: The independent acceptance tester of a GDBox hybrid team. Use when you are the producer and the game is ready to deliver. Do not use to build or fix anything, or outside a GDBox team. ([ueboxai/gdbox](https://github.com/ueboxai/gdbox/blob/HEAD/packages/cli/claude-plugin-hybrid/agents/gdbox-acceptor.md) · ★110)
- **gdbox-acceptor**: The independent acceptance tester of a GDBox studio team. Use when you are the producer of a GDBox external team and the game is ready to deliver. Do not use to build or fix anything, or outside a GDBox team. ([ueboxai/gdbox](https://github.com/ueboxai/gdbox/blob/HEAD/packages/cli/claude-plugin/agents/gdbox-acceptor.md) · ★110)
- **gdbox-member**: A teammate in a GDBox studio team. Use when you are the producer of a GDBox external team (after team_open) and want a teammate you registered with team_hire to do one assignment. - **gdd-auditor**: Independent GDD reviewer. - **gdscript-performance**: GDScript is an interpreter in front of a C++ engine. The expensive work is not usually the `draw_*` call - it is the per-element loop that builds the arguments, the node construction around it, the allocation per cell, and the cross-FFI call per item. This page is the method for finding and proving one of those costs, and the rule for deciding whether the work belongs in Rust. ([godot-pty/gpty](https://github.com/godot-pty/gpty/blob/HEAD/docs/content/docs/agents/gdscript-performance.md) · ★78)
- **gdscript-reviewer**: Use proactively whenever the user asks to review, check or look over GDScript or scene changes in a Godot 4 project (e.g. "review my changes before I commit"), instead of reviewing by reading alone. It backs every finding with evidence from the engine or the code - the parse gate, the engine's own GDScript warnings (a normal headless run never prints them), the static RPC check, and a checklist of measured traps such as un-awaited coroutines and node paths the scene lacks. Read-only - it reports, it never edits. ([hibi/godot-claude-kit](https://github.com/hibi/godot-claude-kit/blob/HEAD/godot-kit/agents/gdscript-reviewer.md) · ★3)
